components/LayerPanel.tsx:  // We reverse layers for display so Top layer is at the top of the list
components/LayerPanel.tsx:  // But dnd-kit works better with the actual order. 
components/LayerPanel.tsx:  // However, the user expects the top item to be the top layer.
components/LayerPanel.tsx:  // So we'll work with a reversed copy for the UI.
components/LayerPanel.tsx:  const displayLayers = [...layers].reverse();
components/LayerPanel.tsx:
components/LayerPanel.tsx:  const handleDragEnd = (event: DragEndEvent) => {
components/LayerPanel.tsx:    const { active, over } = event;
components/LayerPanel.tsx:
components/LayerPanel.tsx:    if (over && active.id !== over.id) {
components/LayerPanel.tsx:      const oldIndex = displayLayers.findIndex((l) => l.id === active.id);
components/LayerPanel.tsx:      const newIndex = displayLayers.findIndex((l) => l.id === over.id);
components/LayerPanel.tsx:      
components/LayerPanel.tsx:      const newDisplayLayers = arrayMove(displayLayers, oldIndex, newIndex);
components/LayerPanel.tsx:      // Reverse back to original internal order (bottom to top)
components/LayerPanel.tsx:      onReorderLayers([...newDisplayLayers].reverse());
components/LayerPanel.tsx:    }
components/LayerPanel.tsx:  };

components/ExportModal.tsx:  const handleShare = async () => {
components/ExportModal.tsx:    if (!exportedFile) return;
components/ExportModal.tsx:
components/ExportModal.tsx:    // 1. Try Median.co / GoNative Bridge (for Android/iOS native apps)

components/ExportModal.tsx:    if (median?.share?.file) {
components/ExportModal.tsx:      try {

components/ExportModal.tsx:          });
components/ExportModal.tsx:        };
components/ExportModal.tsx:        reader.readAsDataURL(exportedFile.blob);
components/ExportModal.tsx:        return;

components/ExportModal.tsx:    // 2. Try Web Share API

components/Waveform.tsx:        const audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)();
components/Waveform.tsx:        const audioBuffer = await audioContext.decodeAudioData(arrayBuffer);
components/Waveform.tsx:        
components/Waveform.tsx:        setOriginalDuration(audioBuffer.duration);
components/Waveform.tsx:        
components/Waveform.tsx:        // Downsample for rendering
components/Waveform.tsx:        const rawData = audioBuffer.getChannelData(0); // Use first channel
components/Waveform.tsx:        const samples = 1000; // Fixed number of samples for the waveform
components/Waveform.tsx:        const blockSize = Math.floor(rawData.length / samples);
components/Waveform.tsx:        const filteredData = new Float32Array(samples);
components/Waveform.tsx:        
components/Waveform.tsx:        for (let i = 0; i < samples; i++) {
components/Waveform.tsx:          let blockStart = blockSize * i;
components/Waveform.tsx:          let sum = 0;
components/Waveform.tsx:          for (let j = 0; j < blockSize; j++) {
components/Waveform.tsx:            sum = sum + Math.abs(rawData[blockStart + j]);
components/Waveform.tsx:          }
components/Waveform.tsx:          filteredData[i] = sum / blockSize;
components/Waveform.tsx:        }
components/Waveform.tsx:        
components/Waveform.tsx:        setAudioData(filteredData);
components/Waveform.tsx:      } catch (error) {
components/Waveform.tsx:        console.error("Error decoding audio for waveform:", error);
components/Waveform.tsx:      }
components/Waveform.tsx:    };
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    fetchAudio();
components/Waveform.tsx:  }, [url]);

components/Waveform.tsx:  useEffect(() => {
components/Waveform.tsx:    if (!canvasRef.current || !audioData || originalDuration === 0) return;
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
components/Waveform.tsx:    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
components/Waveform.tsx:    if (!ctx) return;
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    // Handle high DPI displays
components/Waveform.tsx:    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
components/Waveform.tsx:    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
components/Waveform.tsx:    canvas.width = rect.width * dpr;
components/Waveform.tsx:    canvas.height = rect.height * dpr;
components/Waveform.tsx:    ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    const width = rect.width;
components/Waveform.tsx:    const height = rect.height;
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    // Calculate which portion of the audio data to draw based on offset and duration
components/Waveform.tsx:    const startRatio = offset / originalDuration;
components/Waveform.tsx:    const endRatio = (offset + duration) / originalDuration;
components/Waveform.tsx:    
components/Waveform.tsx:    const startIndex = Math.floor(startRatio * audioData.length);
components/Waveform.tsx:    const endIndex = Math.min(audioData.length, Math.ceil(endRatio * audioData.length));
components/Waveform.tsx:    
components/Waveform.tsx:    const visibleData = audioData.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
components/Waveform.tsx:    if (visibleData.length === 0) return;
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    // Normalize visible data
components/Waveform.tsx:    const maxVal = Math.max(...visibleData);
components/Waveform.tsx:    const multiplier = maxVal > 0 ? Math.pow(maxVal, -1) : 1;
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    ctx.beginPath();
components/Waveform.tsx:    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
components/Waveform.tsx:    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
components/Waveform.tsx:    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:    const step = width / visibleData.length;
components/Waveform.tsx:    
components/Waveform.tsx:    for (let i = 0; i < visibleData.length; i++) {
components/Waveform.tsx:      const x = i * step;
components/Waveform.tsx:      const y = (visibleData[i] * multiplier * height) / 2;
components/Waveform.tsx:      
components/Waveform.tsx:      ctx.moveTo(x, height / 2 - y);
components/Waveform.tsx:      ctx.lineTo(x, height / 2 + y);
components/Waveform.tsx:    }
components/Waveform.tsx:    
components/Waveform.tsx:    ctx.stroke();
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:  }, [audioData, color, duration, offset, originalDuration]);
components/Waveform.tsx:
components/Waveform.tsx:  return (
components/Waveform.tsx:    <canvas 
components/Waveform.tsx:      ref={canvasRef} 
components/Waveform.tsx:    />
components/Waveform.tsx:  );

components/CanvasArea.tsx:        else if (baseAngle === 225) baseAngle = 135;

components/CanvasArea.tsx:        case 2: return 'ew-resize';

components/CanvasArea.tsx:      const sy = (y0 < y1) ? 1 : -1;
components/CanvasArea.tsx:      let err = dx - dy;
components/CanvasArea.tsx:
components/CanvasArea.tsx:      ctx.fillStyle = color;
components/CanvasArea.tsx:
components/CanvasArea.tsx:      let x = x0;
components/CanvasArea.tsx:      let y = y0;
components/CanvasArea.tsx:
components/CanvasArea.tsx:      while(true) {
components/CanvasArea.tsx:          ctx.fillRect(Math.floor(x - size/2), Math.floor(y - size/2), size, size);
components/CanvasArea.tsx:

components/CanvasArea.tsx:          const e2 = 2 * err;
components/CanvasArea.tsx:          if (e2 > -dy) { err -= dy; x += sx; }
components/CanvasArea.tsx:          if (e2 < dx) { err += dx; y += sy; }
components/CanvasArea.tsx:      }

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:export const VideoImportModal: React.FC<VideoImportModalProps> = ({

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const { t } = useTranslation();
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const [videoUrl, setVideoUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const [startTime, setStartTime] = useState(0);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const [endTime, setEndTime] = useState(0);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const [importAudio, setImportAudio] = useState(true);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const [isExtracting, setIsExtracting] = useState(false);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  useEffect(() => {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    if (videoFile) {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      const url = URL.createObjectURL(videoFile);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      setVideoUrl(url);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      return () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    } else {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      setVideoUrl(null);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    }
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  }, [videoFile]);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const handleLoadedMetadata = () => {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    if (videoRef.current) {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      const d = videoRef.current.duration;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      setDuration(d);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      setStartTime(0);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      setEndTime(Math.min(d, 5)); // Default to 5 seconds max to prevent huge imports initially
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    }
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  };
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  const handleExtract = async () => {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    if (!videoRef.current || !canvasRef.current) return;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    setIsExtracting(true);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    setProgress(0);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    const video = videoRef.current;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    const canvas = canvasRef.current;

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    if (!ctx) return;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    // Set canvas size to video resolution

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    const frames: string[] = [];
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    const interval = 1 / targetFps;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    const totalFrames = Math.floor((endTime - startTime) / interval);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    let currentTime = startTime;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    let frameCount = 0;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    const extractFrame = () => {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      return new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:        const onSeeked = () => {
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:          ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:          frames.push(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:          video.removeEventListener('seeked', onSeeked);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:          resolve();
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:        };
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:        video.addEventListener('seeked', onSeeked);
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:        video.currentTime = currentTime;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      });

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    // Save original state

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    if (!originalPaused) video.pause();

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:        setProgress(Math.round((frameCount / totalFrames) * 100));
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:        currentTime += interval;
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:      }
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:    } catch (e) {

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:  };

components/VideoImportModal.tsx:                      step={0.1}
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:                      value={startTime}
components/VideoImportModal.tsx:                      onChange={(e) => {

App.tsx:  // 2. Updated Handle Import Image with Correct GIF Parsing
App.tsx:  const handleImportImage = async (file: File) => {
App.tsx:    const reader = new FileReader();
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    // --- GIF IMPORT LOGIC ---
App.tsx:    if (file.type === 'image/gif') {
App.tsx:        reader.onload = async (e) => {
App.tsx:            const buffer = e.target?.result as ArrayBuffer;
App.tsx:            try {
App.tsx:                // Parse
App.tsx:                const gif = parseGIF(buffer);
App.tsx:                const framesData = decompressFrames(gif, true);
App.tsx:                
App.tsx:                const newFrames: Frame[] = await Promise.all(framesData.map(async (gifFrame: any) => {
App.tsx:                    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:                    canvas.width = canvasSize.width;
App.tsx:                    canvas.height = canvasSize.height;
App.tsx:                    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
App.tsx:                    
App.tsx:                    // Create a temporary canvas to draw the GIF frame "patch"
App.tsx:                    const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:                    tempCanvas.width = gifFrame.dims.width;
App.tsx:                    tempCanvas.height = gifFrame.dims.height;
App.tsx:                    const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d')!;
App.tsx:                    const imgData = tempCtx.createImageData(gifFrame.dims.width, gifFrame.dims.height);
App.tsx:                    
App.tsx:                    // Set the pixel data
App.tsx:                    imgData.data.set(gifFrame.patch);
App.tsx:                    tempCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
App.tsx:                    
App.tsx:                    // Draw onto the main project-sized canvas
App.tsx:                    ctx.drawImage(tempCanvas, gifFrame.dims.left, gifFrame.dims.top);
App.tsx:                    const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL();
App.tsx:                    
App.tsx:                    const layerData: Record<string, string> = {};
App.tsx:                    layers.forEach(l => {
App.tsx:                        // Put the GIF frame on the active layer, blank others
App.tsx:                        if (l.id === activeLayerId) {
App.tsx:                            layerData[l.id] = dataUrl;
App.tsx:                        } else {
App.tsx:                            const blank = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:                            blank.width = canvasSize.width;
App.tsx:                            blank.height = canvasSize.height;
App.tsx:                            layerData[l.id] = blank.toDataURL();
App.tsx:                        }
App.tsx:                    });
App.tsx:
App.tsx:                    const frameObj: Frame = {
App.tsx:                        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
App.tsx:                        layers: layerData,
App.tsx:                        thumbnailUrl: '' // Will be set below
App.tsx:                    };
App.tsx:                    
App.tsx:                    frameObj.thumbnailUrl = await compositeLayers(frameObj, layers, canvasSize.width, canvasSize.height, background, backgroundImage, false);
App.tsx:                    return frameObj;
App.tsx:                }));
App.tsx:
App.tsx:                // Insert all GIF frames into the timeline
App.tsx:                const updatedFrames = [...frames];
App.tsx:                updatedFrames.splice(currentFrameIndex + 1, 0, ...newFrames);
App.tsx:                
App.tsx:                // Update states
App.tsx:                setFrames(updatedFrames);
App.tsx:                setCurrentFrameIndex(currentFrameIndex + 1);
App.tsx:                setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
App.tsx:                
App.tsx:                console.log(`Successfully imported ${newFrames.length} GIF frames`);
App.tsx:            } catch (err) {
App.tsx:                console.error("GIF Error:", err);
App.tsx:                alert(t('errors.gifError'));
App.tsx:            }
App.tsx:        };
App.tsx:        reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
App.tsx:        return;
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    // --- STANDARD IMAGE LOGIC (PNG/JPG) ---
App.tsx:    reader.onload = (e) => {
App.tsx:        const result = e.target?.result as string;
App.tsx:        const img = new Image();
App.tsx:        img.onload = () => {
App.tsx:            const maxWidth = canvasSize.width * 0.8;
App.tsx:            const ratio = img.width / img.height;
App.tsx:            const width = Math.min(img.width, maxWidth);
App.tsx:            const height = width / ratio;
App.tsx:            const x = (canvasSize.width - width) / 2;
App.tsx:            const y = (canvasSize.height - height) / 2;
App.tsx:            setSelection({
App.tsx:              x,
App.tsx:              y,
App.tsx:              width,
App.tsx:              height,
App.tsx:              dataUrl: result,
App.tsx:              rotation: 0,
App.tsx:              scaleX: 1,
App.tsx:              scaleY: 1,
App.tsx:              anchorX: width / 2,
App.tsx:              anchorY: height / 2
App.tsx:            });
App.tsx:            setTool('select');
App.tsx:            setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
App.tsx:        };
App.tsx:        img.src = result;
App.tsx:    };
App.tsx:    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:

App.tsx:              name: file.name + ' (Audio)',
App.tsx:              color: COLORS[audioTracks.length % COLORS.length],

App.tsx:  const handleCutAudioTrack = (id: string, cutTime: number) => {

App.tsx:    if (!track) return;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const relativeCutTime = cutTime - track.startTime;
App.tsx:    if (relativeCutTime <= 0.1 || relativeCutTime >= track.duration - 0.1) return;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const firstPart: AudioTrack = {
App.tsx:        ...track,
App.tsx:        duration: relativeCutTime
App.tsx:    };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const secondPart: AudioTrack = {
App.tsx:        ...track,
App.tsx:        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
App.tsx:        startTime: cutTime,
App.tsx:        duration: track.duration - relativeCutTime,
App.tsx:        offset: track.offset + relativeCutTime
App.tsx:    };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const audio2 = new Audio(track.url);
App.tsx:    audio2.onloadedmetadata = () => {
App.tsx:        audio2.volume = secondPart.volume;
App.tsx:        audioElementsRef.current.set(secondPart.id, audio2);
App.tsx:        setAudioTracks(prev => prev.flatMap(t => t.id === id ? [firstPart, secondPart] : [t]));
App.tsx:        setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
App.tsx:    };
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const handleAddSoundLibraryTrack = async (url: string, name: string) => {
App.tsx:    try {
App.tsx:      const response = await fetch(url);
App.tsx:      const blob = await response.blob();
App.tsx:      const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      const audio = new Audio(objectUrl);
App.tsx:      audio.onloadedmetadata = () => {
App.tsx:        const newTrack: AudioTrack = {
App.tsx:          id: crypto.randomUUID(),
App.tsx:          url: objectUrl,
App.tsx:          name: name,
App.tsx:          color: `hsl(${Math.random() * 360}, 70%, 50%)`,
App.tsx:          volume: 1,
App.tsx:          startTime: currentFrameIndex / fps,
App.tsx:          duration: audio.duration,
App.tsx:          offset: 0
App.tsx:        };

App.tsx:        audioElementsRef.current.set(newTrack.id, audio);
App.tsx:        setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
App.tsx:      };
App.tsx:    } catch (error) {
App.tsx:      console.error("Error adding sound library track:", error);
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const handleRemoveAudioTrack = (id: string) => {
App.tsx:      const trackToRemove = audioTracks.find(t => t.id === id);
App.tsx:      const audio = audioElementsRef.current.get(id);
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      if (audio) {
App.tsx:          audio.pause();
App.tsx:          audioElementsRef.current.delete(id);
App.tsx:          
App.tsx:          // Only revoke if no other track uses this URL
App.tsx:          const isUrlShared = audioTracks.some(t => t.id !== id && t.url === trackToRemove?.url);
App.tsx:          if (!isUrlShared && trackToRemove) {
App.tsx:              URL.revokeObjectURL(trackToRemove.url);
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      setAudioTracks(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id));
App.tsx:      setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const handleSelectionCreate = (newSelection: SelectionState) => {

App.tsx:          createdAt: Date.now()

App.tsx:  const handleSelectionCommit = async () => {
App.tsx:      if (!selection) return;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:      canvas.width = canvasSize.width;
App.tsx:      canvas.height = canvasSize.height;
App.tsx:      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
App.tsx:      if (!ctx) return;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      const layerData = frames[currentFrameIndex].layers[activeLayerId];
App.tsx:      if (layerData) {
App.tsx:          const currentImg = await new Promise<HTMLImageElement>((resolve) => {
App.tsx:              const img = new Image();

App.tsx:              img.src = layerData;

App.tsx:          ctx.drawImage(currentImg, 0, 0);

App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const handleApplyMotionPath = async (path: Point[]) => {
App.tsx:      if (!selection || path.length < 5) return;
App.tsx:      setPendingMotionPath(path);
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const finalizeMotionPath = async (path: Point[], numFrames: number, easing: string) => {
App.tsx:      if (!selection || path.length < 5) return;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      const numFramesToAnimate = numFrames;
App.tsx:      const updatedFrames = [...frames];
App.tsx:      const sampledPath: Point[] = [];
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      const getEasingProgress = (t: number, type: string) => {
App.tsx:        switch (type) {
App.tsx:          case 'ease-in': return t * t;
App.tsx:          case 'ease-out': return t * (2 - t);
App.tsx:          case 'ease-in-out': return t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : -1 + (4 - 2 * t) * t;
App.tsx:          default: return t; // linear
App.tsx:        }
App.tsx:      };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      for (let i = 0; i < numFramesToAnimate; i++) {
App.tsx:          const t = i / (numFramesToAnimate - 1);
App.tsx:          const easedT = getEasingProgress(t, easing);
App.tsx:          const index = easedT * (path.length - 1);
App.tsx:          const low = Math.floor(index);
App.tsx:          const high = Math.ceil(index);
App.tsx:          const frac = index - low;
App.tsx:          
App.tsx:          if (low === high) {
App.tsx:              sampledPath.push(path[low]);
App.tsx:          } else {
App.tsx:              sampledPath.push({
App.tsx:                  x: path[low].x * (1 - frac) + path[high].x * frac,
App.tsx:                  y: path[low].y * (1 - frac) + path[high].y * frac
App.tsx:              });
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      let frameIdx = currentFrameIndex;
App.tsx:      const initialLayerData = updatedFrames[currentFrameIndex].layers[activeLayerId];
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      for (let i = 0; i < sampledPath.length; i++) {
App.tsx:          if (frameIdx >= updatedFrames.length) {
App.tsx:              const lastFrame = updatedFrames[updatedFrames.length - 1];
App.tsx:              const newFrame: Frame = {
App.tsx:                  ...lastFrame,
App.tsx:                  id: crypto.randomUUID(),
App.tsx:                  layers: { 
App.tsx:                      ...lastFrame.layers,
App.tsx:                      [activeLayerId]: initialLayerData // Use the "clean" layer with the hole
App.tsx:                  }
App.tsx:              };
App.tsx:              updatedFrames.push(newFrame);
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          const frame = updatedFrames[frameIdx];
App.tsx:          const point = sampledPath[i];
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:          canvas.width = canvasSize.width;
App.tsx:          canvas.height = canvasSize.height;
App.tsx:          const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
App.tsx:          
App.tsx:          if (ctx) {
App.tsx:              const layerData = frame.layers[activeLayerId];
App.tsx:              if (layerData) {
App.tsx:                  const img = await new Promise<HTMLImageElement>(r => {
App.tsx:                      const img = new Image();
App.tsx:                      img.onload = () => r(img);
App.tsx:                      img.src = layerData;
App.tsx:                  });
App.tsx:                  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
App.tsx:                  
App.tsx:                  // If this is an existing frame (other than the first one), 
App.tsx:                  // it might still have the object. Try to clear it.
App.tsx:                  if (frameIdx > currentFrameIndex && selection.originX !== undefined && selection.originY !== undefined) {
App.tsx:                      ctx.save();
App.tsx:                      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
App.tsx:                      if (selection.maskUrl) {
App.tsx:                          const maskImg = await new Promise<HTMLImageElement>(r => {
App.tsx:                              const img = new Image();
App.tsx:                              img.onload = () => r(img);
App.tsx:                              img.src = selection.maskUrl!;
App.tsx:                          });
App.tsx:                          ctx.drawImage(maskImg, selection.originX, selection.originY, selection.width, selection.height);
App.tsx:                      } else {
App.tsx:                          ctx.fillRect(selection.originX, selection.originY, selection.width, selection.height);
App.tsx:                      }
App.tsx:                      ctx.restore();
App.tsx:                  }
App.tsx:              }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:              const movedSelection = {
App.tsx:                  ...selection,
App.tsx:                  x: point.x - (selection.anchorX ?? selection.width / 2),
App.tsx:                  y: point.y - (selection.anchorY ?? selection.height / 2)
App.tsx:              };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:              await drawSelectionOntoCanvas(ctx, movedSelection);
App.tsx:              updatedFrames[frameIdx] = {
App.tsx:                  ...updatedFrames[frameIdx],
App.tsx:                  layers: {
App.tsx:                      ...updatedFrames[frameIdx].layers,
App.tsx:                      [activeLayerId]: canvas.toDataURL()
App.tsx:                  }
App.tsx:              };
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:          frameIdx++;
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      setFrames(updatedFrames);
App.tsx:      setSelection(null);
App.tsx:      setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
App.tsx:      setTool('pen');
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const handleExportStart = async (format: ExportFormat, quality: ExportQuality) => {
App.tsx:    setIsExporting(true);
App.tsx:    setExportProgress(0);
App.tsx:    isExportCancelledRef.current = false;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const total = frames.length;
App.tsx:    const compositeFrames: string[] = [];
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    // Pre-render all frames
App.tsx:    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
App.tsx:      if (isExportCancelledRef.current) {
App.tsx:          setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:          return;
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:      const dataUrl = await compositeLayers(frames[i], layers, canvasSize.width, canvasSize.height, background, backgroundImage);
App.tsx:      compositeFrames.push(dataUrl);
App.tsx:      setExportProgress(Math.round(((i + 1) / total) * 30));
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    if (format === 'mp4') {
App.tsx:        try {
App.tsx:            // Ensure even dimensions for H.264
App.tsx:            const exportWidth = canvasSize.width % 2 === 0 ? canvasSize.width : canvasSize.width - 1;
App.tsx:            const exportHeight = canvasSize.height % 2 === 0 ? canvasSize.height : canvasSize.height - 1;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            // Using mp4-muxer for valid MP4 generation
App.tsx:            const muxer = new Mp4Muxer.Muxer({
App.tsx:                target: new Mp4Muxer.ArrayBufferTarget(),
App.tsx:                video: {
App.tsx:                    codec: 'avc',
App.tsx:                    width: exportWidth,
App.tsx:                    height: exportHeight
App.tsx:                },
App.tsx:                fastStart: 'in-memory',
App.tsx:                firstTimestampBehavior: 'offset',
App.tsx:            });
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            const videoEncoder = new VideoEncoder({
App.tsx:                output: (chunk, meta) => muxer.addVideoChunk(chunk, meta),
App.tsx:                error: (e) => { console.error(e); alert(t('errors.exportError', { message: e.message })); }
App.tsx:            });
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            const bitrateMap = {
App.tsx:                low: 1_000_000,
App.tsx:                medium: 4_000_000,
App.tsx:                high: 10_000_000
App.tsx:            };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            videoEncoder.configure({
App.tsx:                codec: 'avc1.42001f', // Standard AVC
App.tsx:                width: exportWidth,
App.tsx:                height: exportHeight,
App.tsx:                bitrate: bitrateMap[quality],
App.tsx:                framerate: fps
App.tsx:            });
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            // Loop frames
App.tsx:            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:            canvas.width = exportWidth;
App.tsx:            canvas.height = exportHeight;
App.tsx:            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
App.tsx:            if (!ctx) throw new Error("No context");
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            for (let i = 0; i < compositeFrames.length; i++) {
App.tsx:                 if (isExportCancelledRef.current) {
App.tsx:                     setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:                     return;
App.tsx:                 }
App.tsx:                 const img = new Image();
App.tsx:                 await new Promise<void>((resolve) => { img.onload = () => resolve(); img.src = compositeFrames[i]; });
App.tsx:                 
App.tsx:                 ctx.clearRect(0, 0, exportWidth, exportHeight);
App.tsx:                 if (background.type === 'gradient3' && background.gradientColors) {
App.tsx:                    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, exportWidth, exportHeight);
App.tsx:                    gradient.addColorStop(0, background.gradientColors[0]);
App.tsx:                    gradient.addColorStop(0.5, background.gradientColors[1]);
App.tsx:                    gradient.addColorStop(1, background.gradientColors[2]);
App.tsx:                    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
App.tsx:                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, exportWidth, exportHeight);
App.tsx:                 } else if (background.color !== 'transparent') {
App.tsx:                    ctx.fillStyle = background.color;
App.tsx:                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, exportWidth, exportHeight);
App.tsx:                 }
App.tsx:                 ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, exportWidth, exportHeight);
App.tsx:
App.tsx:                 const frame = new VideoFrame(canvas, { timestamp: i * 1000000 / fps });
App.tsx:                 videoEncoder.encode(frame);
App.tsx:                 frame.close();
App.tsx:
App.tsx:                 setExportProgress(30 + Math.round(((i + 1) / total) * 60));
App.tsx:            }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            await videoEncoder.flush();
App.tsx:            muxer.finalize();
App.tsx:
App.tsx:            const { buffer } = muxer.target;
App.tsx:            const blob = new Blob([buffer], { type: 'video/mp4' });
App.tsx:            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
App.tsx:            
App.tsx:            setExportedFile({ url, name: `${projectName}.mp4`, blob });
App.tsx:            setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:            setExportProgress(100);
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        } catch (e: any) {
App.tsx:            console.error("MP4 Export failed", e);
App.tsx:            alert(t('errors.mp4Error'));
App.tsx:            setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:            setIsExportModalOpen(false);
App.tsx:        }
App.tsx:    } else if (format === 'gif') {
App.tsx:      try {
App.tsx:        const qualityMap = {
App.tsx:            low: 30,
App.tsx:            medium: 10,
App.tsx:            high: 1
App.tsx:        };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        const gifBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
App.tsx:          gifshot.createGIF({
App.tsx:            images: compositeFrames,
App.tsx:            gifWidth: canvasSize.width,
App.tsx:            gifHeight: canvasSize.height,
App.tsx:            frameDuration: 1 / fps, // gifshot uses seconds per frame
App.tsx:            numWorkers: 2, // Use more workers for faster processing
App.tsx:            quality: qualityMap[quality]
App.tsx:          }, (obj: any) => {
App.tsx:            if (obj.error) {
App.tsx:              reject(obj.error);
App.tsx:            } else {
App.tsx:              fetch(obj.image).then(res => res.blob()).then(resolve).catch(reject);
App.tsx:            }
App.tsx:          });
App.tsx:        });
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        const url = URL.createObjectURL(gifBlob);
App.tsx:        
App.tsx:        setExportedFile({ url, name: `${projectName}.gif`, blob: gifBlob });
App.tsx:        setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:        setExportProgress(100);
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      } catch (e: any) {
App.tsx:        console.error("GIF Export failed", e);
App.tsx:        alert(t('errors.exportError', { message: e.message }));
App.tsx:        setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:        setIsExportModalOpen(false);
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:    } else if (format === 'png-seq') {
App.tsx:      const zip = new JSZip();
App.tsx:      compositeFrames.forEach((data, i) => {
App.tsx:        const base64Data = data.split(',')[1];
App.tsx:        zip.file(`frame_${String(i + 1).padStart(4, '0')}.png`, base64Data, { base64: true });
App.tsx:      });
App.tsx:      const content = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob' }, (metadata: any) => {
App.tsx:        setExportProgress(30 + Math.round(metadata.percent * 0.7));
App.tsx:      });
App.tsx:      const url = URL.createObjectURL(content);
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      setExportedFile({ url, name: `${projectName}_frames.zip`, blob: content });
App.tsx:      setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:      setExportProgress(100);
App.tsx:    } else {
App.tsx:      // Fallback for WebM/AVI using MediaRecorder
App.tsx:      try {
App.tsx:          const exportCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:          exportCanvas.width = canvasSize.width;
App.tsx:          exportCanvas.height = canvasSize.height;
App.tsx:          const ctx = exportCanvas.getContext('2d');
App.tsx:          
App.tsx:          if(!ctx) throw new Error("Could not create canvas context");
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          const stream = exportCanvas.captureStream(fps);
App.tsx:          
App.tsx:          const mimeTypes: Record<string, string[]> = {
App.tsx:            webm: ['video/webm;codecs=vp9', 'video/webm'],
App.tsx:            avi: ['video/webm'] // Hack: Browsers don't support AVI encoding
App.tsx:          };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          const selectedMimeType = mimeTypes[format]?.find(type => MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(type)) || 'video/webm';
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          const bitrateMap = {
App.tsx:              low: 1_000_000,
App.tsx:              medium: 8_000_000,
App.tsx:              high: 20_000_000
App.tsx:          };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, {
App.tsx:              mimeType: selectedMimeType,
App.tsx:              videoBitsPerSecond: bitrateMap[quality]
App.tsx:          });
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          const chunks: BlobPart[] = [];
App.tsx:          mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
App.tsx:              if (e.data.size > 0) chunks.push(e.data);
App.tsx:          };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
App.tsx:              const blob = new Blob(chunks, { type: selectedMimeType });
App.tsx:              const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
App.tsx:              const ext = format === 'avi' ? 'avi' : 'webm';
App.tsx:              
App.tsx:              setExportedFile({ url, name: `${projectName}.${ext}`, blob });
App.tsx:              setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:              setExportProgress(100);
App.tsx:          };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          mediaRecorder.start();
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          for (let i = 0; i < compositeFrames.length; i++) {
App.tsx:              if (isExportCancelledRef.current) {
App.tsx:                  mediaRecorder.stop();
App.tsx:                  setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:                  return;
App.tsx:              }
App.tsx:              const img = new Image();
App.tsx:              await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
App.tsx:                  img.onload = () => resolve();
App.tsx:                  img.src = compositeFrames[i];
App.tsx:              });
App.tsx:              
App.tsx:              ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
App.tsx:              ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvasSize.width, canvasSize.height);
App.tsx:              ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
App.tsx:              
App.tsx:              await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000 / fps));
App.tsx:              setExportProgress(30 + Math.round(((i + 1) / total) * 70));
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:          
App.tsx:          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));
App.tsx:          mediaRecorder.stop();
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      } catch (e: any) {
App.tsx:          console.error(e);
App.tsx:          alert(t('errors.exportError', { message: e.message }));
App.tsx:          setIsExporting(false);
App.tsx:          setIsExportModalOpen(false);
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:  };

App.tsx:  const loadProject = async (id: string) => {

App.tsx:  const handleImportIntoSelection = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {

App.tsx:    if (!file || !selection) return;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const reader = new FileReader();

App.tsx:

App.tsx:          const img = new Image();

App.tsx:      };
App.tsx:

App.tsx:        ]);
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:        canvas.width = selection.width;
App.tsx:        canvas.height = selection.height;
App.tsx:        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
App.tsx:        if (!ctx) return;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        // 1. Draw the imported image first (scaled to cover)
App.tsx:        const scale = Math.max(selection.width / importedImg.width, selection.height / importedImg.height);
App.tsx:        const drawWidth = importedImg.width * scale;
App.tsx:        const drawHeight = importedImg.height * scale;
App.tsx:        const offsetX = (selection.width - drawWidth) / 2;
App.tsx:        const offsetY = (selection.height - drawHeight) / 2;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        ctx.drawImage(importedImg, offsetX, offsetY, drawWidth, drawHeight);
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        // 2. Load the mask image
App.tsx:        let maskImgToUse: HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement = maskImg;
App.tsx:        
App.tsx:        if (selection.selectionType === 'rectangle') {
App.tsx:          const maskCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:          maskCanvas.width = selection.width;
App.tsx:          maskCanvas.height = selection.height;
App.tsx:          const maskCtx = maskCanvas.getContext('2d');
App.tsx:          if (maskCtx) {
App.tsx:            maskCtx.fillStyle = '#000';
App.tsx:            maskCtx.fillRect(0, 0, selection.width, selection.height);
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:          maskImgToUse = maskCanvas;
App.tsx:        } else if (selection.maskUrl) {
App.tsx:          try {
App.tsx:            maskImgToUse = await loadImage(selection.maskUrl);
App.tsx:          } catch (e) {
App.tsx:            console.warn("Failed to load maskUrl, falling back to dataUrl", e);
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:        }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        // 3. Apply the mask using destination-in
App.tsx:        // This keeps the imported image only where the mask is opaque
App.tsx:        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
App.tsx:        ctx.drawImage(maskImgToUse, 0, 0, selection.width, selection.height);
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        // 4. Force a small delay to ensure canvas is updated before toDataURL
App.tsx:        // This can help on some mobile browsers
App.tsx:        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        const newDataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        setSelection({
App.tsx:          ...selection,
App.tsx:          dataUrl: newDataUrl
App.tsx:        });
App.tsx:        setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
App.tsx:      } catch (err) {
App.tsx:        console.error("Error importing into selection:", err);
App.tsx:      }

App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const animate = (timestamp: number) => {
App.tsx:    if (!isPlaying) return;
App.tsx:    
App.tsx:    if (startTimeRef.current === 0) startTimeRef.current = timestamp;
App.tsx:    let elapsed = (timestamp - startTimeRef.current) / 1000;
App.tsx:    const totalDuration = frameTimings.length > 0 ? frameTimings[frameTimings.length - 1].end : 0;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    if (elapsed >= totalDuration) { 
App.tsx:      if (isLooping) {
App.tsx:        startTimeRef.current = timestamp;
App.tsx:        elapsed = 0;
App.tsx:        setCurrentFrameIndex(0);
App.tsx:      } else {
App.tsx:        setIsPlaying(false); 
App.tsx:        return; 
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const targetFrame = frameTimings.findIndex((t: { start: number, end: number }) => elapsed >= t.start && elapsed < t.end);
App.tsx:    if (targetFrame !== -1 && targetFrame !== currentFrameIndex) {
App.tsx:      setCurrentFrameIndex(targetFrame);
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    // Audio Sync
App.tsx:    audioTracks.forEach(track => {
App.tsx:        const audio = audioElementsRef.current.get(track.id);
App.tsx:        if (audio) {
App.tsx:            const trackEndTime = track.startTime + track.duration;
App.tsx:            if (elapsed >= track.startTime && elapsed < trackEndTime) {
App.tsx:                // Volume & Fade logic
App.tsx:                let volume = track.volume;
App.tsx:                const timeInTrack = elapsed - track.startTime;
App.tsx:                
App.tsx:                if (track.fadeIn && timeInTrack < track.fadeIn) {
App.tsx:                    volume *= (timeInTrack / track.fadeIn);
App.tsx:                } else if (track.fadeOut && (track.duration - timeInTrack) < track.fadeOut) {
App.tsx:                    volume *= ((track.duration - timeInTrack) / track.fadeOut);
App.tsx:                }
App.tsx:                
App.tsx:                if (audio.volume !== volume) {
App.tsx:                    // Smooth volume adjustment to avoid pops
App.tsx:                    audio.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));
App.tsx:                }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:                if (audio.paused) {
App.tsx:                    audio.currentTime = track.offset + (elapsed - track.startTime);
App.tsx:                    audio.play().catch(e => {
App.tsx:                        if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.error(e);
App.tsx:                    });
App.tsx:                } else {
App.tsx:                    const expectedTime = track.offset + (elapsed - track.startTime);
App.tsx:                    if (Math.abs(audio.currentTime - expectedTime) > 0.15) {
App.tsx:                        audio.currentTime = expectedTime;
App.tsx:                    }
App.tsx:                }
App.tsx:            } else {
App.tsx:                if (!audio.paused) audio.pause();
App.tsx:            }
App.tsx:        }
App.tsx:    });
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    requestRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  useEffect(() => {
App.tsx:    if (isPlaying) {
App.tsx:      const startTime = frameTimings[currentFrameIndex]?.start || 0;
App.tsx:      audioTracks.forEach(track => {
App.tsx:          const audio = audioElementsRef.current.get(track.id);
App.tsx:          if (audio) { 
App.tsx:              const trackEndTime = track.startTime + track.duration;
App.tsx:              if (startTime >= track.startTime && startTime < trackEndTime) {
App.tsx:                  audio.currentTime = track.offset + (startTime - track.startTime);
App.tsx:                  audio.play().catch(e => {
App.tsx:                      if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.error(e);
App.tsx:                  });
App.tsx:              } else {
App.tsx:                  audio.pause();
App.tsx:              }
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:      });
App.tsx:      startTimeRef.current = performance.now() - (startTime * 1000);
App.tsx:      requestRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
App.tsx:    } else {
App.tsx:      if (requestRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(requestRef.current);
App.tsx:      audioElementsRef.current.forEach(audio => audio.pause());
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:    return () => { if (requestRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(requestRef.current); };
App.tsx:  }, [isPlaying, fps]);
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  const handleSelectFrame = (index: number) => {
App.tsx:    setCurrentFrameIndex(index);
App.tsx:    if (isPlaying) {
App.tsx:      startTimeRef.current = performance.now() - (index / fps * 1000);
App.tsx:    }

App.tsx:                const trackEndTime = track.startTime + track.duration;
App.tsx:                if (time >= track.startTime && time < trackEndTime) {
App.tsx:                    audio.currentTime = track.offset + (time - track.startTime);
App.tsx:                    if (isPlaying && audio.paused) {
App.tsx:                        audio.play().catch(e => {
App.tsx:                            if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.error(e);
App.tsx:                        });
App.tsx:                    }
App.tsx:                } else {
App.tsx:                    if (!audio.paused) audio.pause();
App.tsx:                }
App.tsx:            }

App.tsx:  const executeTween = async (

App.tsx:    numTweens: number, 
App.tsx:    easing: string = 'linear',

App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const getEasingProgress = (t: number, type: string) => {
App.tsx:      switch (type) {
App.tsx:        case 'ease-in': return t * t;
App.tsx:        case 'ease-out': return t * (2 - t);
App.tsx:        case 'ease-in-out': return t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : -1 + (4 - 2 * t) * t;
App.tsx:        default: return t; // linear
App.tsx:      }

App.tsx:      const data = imageData.data;
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      let minX = width, minY = height, maxX = -1, maxY = -1;
App.tsx:      let m00 = 0, m10 = 0, m01 = 0, m11 = 0, m20 = 0, m02 = 0;
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
App.tsx:        for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
App.tsx:          const alpha = data[(y * width + x) * 4 + 3];
App.tsx:          if (alpha > 0) {
App.tsx:            const weight = alpha / 255;
App.tsx:            if (x < minX) minX = x;
App.tsx:            if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
App.tsx:            if (y < minY) minY = y;
App.tsx:            if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
App.tsx:            
App.tsx:            m00 += weight;
App.tsx:            m10 += x * weight;
App.tsx:            m01 += y * weight;
App.tsx:            m11 += x * y * weight;
App.tsx:            m20 += x * x * weight;
App.tsx:            m02 += y * y * weight;
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:        }
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      if (m00 < 0.1) return null;
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      const centerX = m10 / m00;
App.tsx:      const centerY = m01 / m00;
App.tsx:      const mu20 = m20 / m00 - centerX * centerX;
App.tsx:      const mu02 = m02 / m00 - centerY * centerY;
App.tsx:      const mu11 = m11 / m00 - centerX * centerY;
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      // Orientation angle in radians (principal axis)
App.tsx:      const angle = 0.5 * Math.atan2(2 * mu11, mu20 - mu02);
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      return { 
App.tsx:        x: minX, 
App.tsx:        y: minY, 
App.tsx:        w: Math.max(1, maxX - minX + 1), 
App.tsx:        h: Math.max(1, maxY - minY + 1),
App.tsx:        centerX,
App.tsx:        centerY,
App.tsx:        angle
App.tsx:      };
App.tsx:    };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const layerData: Record<string, { 
App.tsx:      imgA: HTMLImageElement | null, 
App.tsx:      imgB: HTMLImageElement | null, 
App.tsx:      statsA: any, 
App.tsx:      statsB: any 
App.tsx:    }> = {};
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    for (const layer of layers) {
App.tsx:      let imgA: HTMLImageElement | null = null;
App.tsx:      let imgB: HTMLImageElement | null = null;
App.tsx:      let statsA = null;
App.tsx:      let statsB = null;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      if (frameA.layers[layer.id]) {
App.tsx:        imgA = new Image();
App.tsx:        imgA.src = frameA.layers[layer.id];
App.tsx:        await new Promise(resolve => { imgA!.onload = resolve; });
App.tsx:        statsA = getLayerStats(imgA, canvasSize.width, canvasSize.height);
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      if (frameB.layers[layer.id]) {
App.tsx:        imgB = new Image();
App.tsx:        imgB.src = frameB.layers[layer.id];
App.tsx:        await new Promise(resolve => { imgB!.onload = resolve; });
App.tsx:        statsB = getLayerStats(imgB, canvasSize.width, canvasSize.height);
App.tsx:      }

App.tsx:      layerData[layer.id] = { imgA, imgB, statsA, statsB };
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const compositeCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
App.tsx:    compositeCanvas.width = canvasSize.width;
App.tsx:    compositeCanvas.height = canvasSize.height;
App.tsx:    const compositeCtx = compositeCanvas.getContext('2d');
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    for (let i = 1; i <= numTweens; i++) {
App.tsx:      const t = i / (numTweens + 1);
App.tsx:      const progress = getEasingProgress(t, easing);
App.tsx:      const newLayers: Record<string, string> = {};
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      if (compositeCtx) {
App.tsx:        compositeCtx.clearRect(0, 0, compositeCanvas.width, compositeCanvas.height);
App.tsx:        // Background is handled by Timeline and CanvasArea separately, 
App.tsx:        // thumbnails for onion skinning should be transparent.
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:      for (const layer of layers) {
App.tsx:        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
App.tsx:        
App.tsx:        const { imgA, imgB, statsA, statsB } = layerData[layer.id];
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        if (imgA && imgB && statsA && statsB) {
App.tsx:          // Interpolate stats
App.tsx:          const centerX = interpolatePosition ? (statsA.centerX + (statsB.centerX - statsA.centerX) * progress) : statsA.centerX;
App.tsx:          const centerY = interpolatePosition ? (statsA.centerY + (statsB.centerY - statsA.centerY) * progress) : statsA.centerY;
App.tsx:          const width = interpolateScale ? (statsA.w + (statsB.w - statsA.w) * progress) : statsA.w;
App.tsx:          const height = interpolateScale ? (statsA.h + (statsB.h - statsA.h) * progress) : statsA.h;
App.tsx:          
App.tsx:          // Shortest path for principal axis angle (-PI/2 to PI/2)
App.tsx:          let angle = statsA.angle;
App.tsx:          if (interpolateRotation) {
App.tsx:            let diff = statsB.angle - statsA.angle;
App.tsx:            while (diff > Math.PI / 2) diff -= Math.PI;
App.tsx:            while (diff < -Math.PI / 2) diff += Math.PI;
App.tsx:            angle = statsA.angle + diff * progress;
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          // Cross-fade with smooth transformation
App.tsx:          // Draw imgA
App.tsx:          ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - progress;
App.tsx:          ctx.save();
App.tsx:          ctx.translate(centerX, centerY);
App.tsx:          ctx.rotate(angle - statsA.angle);
App.tsx:          ctx.scale(width / statsA.w, height / statsA.h);
App.tsx:          ctx.translate(-statsA.centerX, -statsA.centerY);
App.tsx:          ctx.drawImage(imgA, 0, 0);
App.tsx:          ctx.restore();
App.tsx:
App.tsx:          // Draw imgB
App.tsx:          ctx.globalAlpha = progress;
App.tsx:          ctx.save();
App.tsx:          ctx.translate(centerX, centerY);
App.tsx:          ctx.rotate(angle - statsB.angle);
App.tsx:          ctx.scale(width / statsB.w, height / statsB.h);
App.tsx:          ctx.translate(-statsB.centerX, -statsB.centerY);
App.tsx:          ctx.drawImage(imgB, 0, 0);
App.tsx:          ctx.restore();
App.tsx:        } else if (imgA || imgB) {
App.tsx:          // Fade in or fade out if only one exists
App.tsx:          ctx.globalAlpha = imgA ? (1 - progress) : progress;
App.tsx:          const targetImg = imgA || imgB;
App.tsx:          if (targetImg) {
App.tsx:            ctx.drawImage(targetImg, 0, 0);
App.tsx:          }
App.tsx:        }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:        ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;
App.tsx:        const layerDataUrl = canvas.toDataURL();
App.tsx:        newLayers[layer.id] = layerDataUrl;
App.tsx:        
App.tsx:        if (compositeCtx && layer.isVisible) {
App.tsx:          compositeCtx.save();
App.tsx:          compositeCtx.globalAlpha = layer.opacity;
App.tsx:          // Use the canvas directly to avoid async image loading issues
App.tsx:          compositeCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);
App.tsx:          compositeCtx.restore();
App.tsx:        }
App.tsx:      }
App.tsx:      
App.tsx:      generatedFrames.push({
App.tsx:        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
App.tsx:        layers: newLayers,
App.tsx:        durationMultiplier: 1,
App.tsx:        background: frameA.background,
App.tsx:        backgroundImage: frameA.backgroundImage,
App.tsx:        thumbnailUrl: compositeCanvas.toDataURL('image/png')
App.tsx:      });
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:
App.tsx:    const newFrames = [...frames];
App.tsx:    newFrames.splice(index + 1, 0, ...generatedFrames);
App.tsx:    updateFramesWithHistory(newFrames);
App.tsx:    setCurrentFrameIndex(index + numTweens + 1);
App.tsx:    setHasUnsavedChanges(true);

App.tsx:    if (!includeOnionSkin) {
App.tsx:        setOnionSkin(originalOnionSkin);
App.tsx:    }
App.tsx:  };
App.tsx:
App.tsx:  // Bulk operations
App.tsx:  const handleBulkDeleteFrames = (indices: number[]) => {
App.tsx:      // If deleting all, keep one blank frame
App.tsx:      let newFrames = frames.filter((_, i) => !indices.includes(i));

App.tsx:

App.tsx:              let newHeight = oldHeight;
App.tsx:

App.tsx:                newWidth = oldWidth;
App.tsx:                newHeight = oldWidth / newAspect;
App.tsx:              } else {
App.tsx:                newHeight = oldHeight;
App.tsx:                newWidth = oldHeight * newAspect;
App.tsx:              }

App.tsx:              const oldAnchorX = selection.anchorX ?? oldWidth / 2;
App.tsx:              const oldAnchorY = selection.anchorY ?? oldHeight / 2;

App.tsx:              const relAnchorY = oldAnchorY / oldHeight;
App.tsx:              
App.tsx:              const newAnchorX = newWidth * relAnchorX;
App.tsx:              const newAnchorY = newHeight * relAnchorY;
App.tsx:
App.tsx:              const newX = selection.x + oldAnchorX - newAnchorX;
App.tsx:              const newY = selection.y + oldAnchorY - newAnchorY;
App.tsx:

App.tsx:                ...selection,
App.tsx:                x: newX,
App.tsx:                y: newY,
App.tsx:                width: newWidth,
App.tsx:                height: newHeight,
App.tsx:                dataUrl: item.dataUrl,
App.tsx:                anchorX: newAnchorX,
App.tsx:                anchorY: newAnchorY
App.tsx:              });

App.tsx:              });

CHANGELOG.md:# Changelog
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.8] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:### Added
CHANGELOG.md:- Camera Mode: A new feature to visualize canvas boundaries and dim the workspace outside the canvas, similar to Adobe products.
CHANGELOG.md:- Improved canvas visualization with a dedicated Camera toggle button.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.7] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Added
CHANGELOG.md:- **Backpack Export:** Added the ability to export backpack items as PNG images or ZIP archives.
CHANGELOG.md:- **Multi-select Export:** Users can now select multiple items in the backpack and download them as a single ZIP file.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.6] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Added
CHANGELOG.md:- **Eyedropper Tool:** A new tool that allows users to pick colors directly from any part of the canvas, including layers and the background.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.5] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Fixed
CHANGELOG.md:- **Backpack Rename:** Fixed an issue where clicking the rename button would accidentally trigger the item selection (spawning it into the canvas). Added event propagation protection and improved layering.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.4] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Fixed
CHANGELOG.md:- **Background Controls:** Added a prominent "Remove Background Image" button in settings for better accessibility, especially on mobile devices where hover states are not available.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.3] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Fixed
CHANGELOG.md:- **Backpack Accessibility:** Increased the touch target and icon size for renaming saved selections in the backpack, making it easier to use on mobile devices.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.2] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Fixed
CHANGELOG.md:- **Mobile Accessibility:** Enabled Zoom In, Zoom Out, and Reset View buttons for Android and other mobile devices.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.1] - 2026-03-31
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Fixed
CHANGELOG.md:- **Canvas Background Rendering:** Frame-specific background settings now correctly override global project settings on the main canvas.
CHANGELOG.md:- **Thumbnail Consistency:** Background colors, gradients, and images are now correctly rendered in both Frame Manager and Timeline thumbnails.
CHANGELOG.md:- **Timeline Background Image Support:** Background images are now correctly displayed behind frame thumbnails in the main timeline.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Changed
CHANGELOG.md:- **Standardized Thumbnail Generation:** Thumbnails are now generated with transparent backgrounds for better Onion Skinning support and UI consistency.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:## [1.1.0] - 2026-03-30
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Added
CHANGELOG.md:- **Freesound Integration:** Users can now search and add thousands of high-quality sound effects directly from Freesound.org.
CHANGELOG.md:- **Server-Side Proxy:** Implemented a backend proxy server (`server.ts`) to handle API requests securely and bypass browser CORS restrictions.
CHANGELOG.md:- **Vercel Support:** Added `vercel.json` and refactored the server to support seamless deployment on Vercel as a full-stack application.
CHANGELOG.md:- **Sound Library UI:** New modal for searching sounds with real-time previews and "Add to Timeline" functionality.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Fixed
CHANGELOG.md:- **CORS Errors:** Resolved "Failed to fetch" issues by routing API calls through the backend.
CHANGELOG.md:- **API Key Security:** Moved sensitive API keys to the server-side environment.
CHANGELOG.md:- **Sound Previews:** Improved preview detection to support multiple audio formats (MP3, OGG).
CHANGELOG.md:- **Error Handling:** Added detailed error reporting for API connection and authentication issues.
CHANGELOG.md:
CHANGELOG.md:### Changed
CHANGELOG.md:- Converted project from a client-side SPA to a Full-Stack Express + Vite application.
CHANGELOG.md:- Updated build and dev scripts to support the new server-side architecture.

server.ts:import express, { Request, Response } from 'express';
server.ts:import path from 'node:path';
server.ts:
server.ts:const app = express();
server.ts:
server.ts:// API Route: Freesound Proxy
server.ts:app.get('/api/search-sounds', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
server.ts:  const { query } = req.query;
server.ts:  console.log(`[SoundSearch] Request for: "${query}"`);
server.ts:  
server.ts:  const apiKey = process.env.FREESOUND_API_KEY || process.env.VITE_FREESOUND_API_KEY;
server.ts:
server.ts:  if (!query) {
server.ts:    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Query parameter is required' });
server.ts:  }
server.ts:
server.ts:  if (!apiKey) {
server.ts:    console.error('[SoundSearch] API Key missing');
server.ts:    return res.status(500).json({ 
server.ts:      error: 'Freesound API key not configured. Please add FREESOUND_API_KEY to your environment variables.' 
server.ts:    });
server.ts:  }
server.ts:
server.ts:  try {
server.ts:    // Use Authorization header instead of query param for better security/compatibility
server.ts:    const freesoundUrl = `https://freesound.org/apiv2/search/text/?query=${encodeURIComponent(query as string)}&fields=id,name,previews,description&page_size=30`;
server.ts:    
server.ts:    console.log(`[SoundSearch] Fetching from Freesound...`);
server.ts:    
server.ts:    const controller = new AbortController();
server.ts:    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
server.ts:      console.log('[SoundSearch] Timeout triggered');
server.ts:      controller.abort();
server.ts:    }, 9000);
server.ts:
server.ts:    try {
server.ts:      const response = await fetch(freesoundUrl, {
server.ts:        signal: controller.signal,
server.ts:        headers: {
server.ts:          'Authorization': `Token ${apiKey}`,
server.ts:          'User-Agent': 'ClipAnimCreator/1.1.0 (https://github.com/your-username/your-repo)'
server.ts:        }
server.ts:      });
server.ts:      
server.ts:      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
server.ts:      console.log(`[SoundSearch] Status: ${response.status}`);
server.ts:      
server.ts:      const text = await response.text();
server.ts:      
server.ts:      let data;
server.ts:      try {
server.ts:        data = JSON.parse(text);
server.ts:      } catch (e) {
server.ts:        console.error('[SoundSearch] JSON Parse Error');
server.ts:        return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Invalid response from Freesound' });
server.ts:      }
server.ts:
server.ts:      if (!response.ok) {
server.ts:        console.error('[SoundSearch] Freesound Error:', data);
server.ts:        return res.status(response.status).json(data);
server.ts:      }
server.ts:
server.ts:      res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=3600');
server.ts:      res.json(data);
server.ts:    } catch (fetchError: any) {
server.ts:      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
server.ts:      throw fetchError;
server.ts:    }
server.ts:  } catch (error: any) {
server.ts:    console.error('[SoundSearch] Global Error:', error.message);
server.ts:    if (error.name === 'AbortError') {
server.ts:      return res.status(504).json({ error: 'Freesound API timed out' });
server.ts:    }
server.ts:    res.status(500).json({ error: `Server Error: ${error.message}` });
server.ts:  }
server.ts:});

utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const hexToRgba = (hex: string) => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const r = parseInt(hex.slice(1, 3), 16);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const g = parseInt(hex.slice(3, 5), 16);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const b = parseInt(hex.slice(5, 7), 16);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  return { r, g, b, a: 255 };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:};
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const hexToHsv = (hex: string) => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    let {r, g, b} = hexToRgba(hex);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    r /= 255; g /= 255; b /= 255;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const max = Math.max(r, g, b), min = Math.min(r, g, b);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    let h = 0, s, v = max;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const d = max - min;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    s = max === 0 ? 0 : d / max;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (max !== min) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        switch (max) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            case r: h = (g - b) / d + (g < b ? 6 : 0); break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            case g: h = (b - r) / d + 2; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            case b: h = (r - g) / d + 4; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        h /= 6;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    return { h: h * 360, s: s * 100, v: v * 100 };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:};
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const hsvToHex = (h: number, s: number, v: number) => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    s /= 100;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    v /= 100;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const i = Math.floor(h / 60);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const f = h / 60 - i;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const p = v * (1 - s);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const q = v * (1 - f * s);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const t = v * (1 - (1 - f) * s);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    switch (i % 6) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        case 0: r = v; g = t; b = p; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        case 1: r = q; g = v; b = p; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        case 2: r = p; g = v; b = t; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        case 3: r = p; g = q; b = v; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        case 4: r = t; g = p; b = v; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        case 5: r = v; g = p; b = q; break;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const toHex = (x: number) => Math.round(x * 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    return `#${toHex(r)}${toHex(g)}${toHex(b)}`;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:};

utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const compositeLayers = async (
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  frame: Frame, 
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  layers: Layer[], 
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  width: number = 800, 
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  height: number = 600,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  background: BackgroundSettings = { type: 'color', color: '#ffffff' },
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  backgroundImage?: string | null,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  includeBackground: boolean = true
utils/drawingUtils.ts:): Promise<string> => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  canvas.width = width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  canvas.height = height;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (!ctx) return '';
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const effectiveBackground = frame.background || background;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const effectiveBackgroundImage = frame.backgroundImage !== undefined ? frame.backgroundImage : backgroundImage;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // 1. Draw Background Image (if exists) or Color/Gradient
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (includeBackground) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (effectiveBackgroundImage) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:       // If we have a background image, draw it to fill the canvas
utils/drawingUtils.ts:       await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          const img = new Image();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          img.onload = () => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:               ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:               resolve();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          img.onerror = () => resolve();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          img.src = effectiveBackgroundImage;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:       });
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    } else {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        // Fallback to background color/gradient
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        if (effectiveBackground.type === 'gradient3' && effectiveBackground.gradientColors) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            gradient.addColorStop(0, effectiveBackground.gradientColors[0]);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            gradient.addColorStop(0.5, effectiveBackground.gradientColors[1]);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            gradient.addColorStop(1, effectiveBackground.gradientColors[2]);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        } else if (effectiveBackground.color === 'transparent') {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        } else {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.fillStyle = effectiveBackground.color;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  } else {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Draw layers in order
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  for (const layer of layers) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (layer.isVisible && frame.layers[layer.id]) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        const img = new Image();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        img.onload = () => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.save();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.globalAlpha = layer.opacity;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.globalCompositeOperation = layer.blendMode;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0); // Layers are drawn at 0,0 relative to canvas size
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          ctx.restore();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          resolve();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        img.onerror = () => resolve(); // Skip if fail
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        img.src = frame.layers[layer.id];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      });
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  return canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:};

utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const drawSelectionOntoCanvas = async (

utils/drawingUtils.ts:      const anchorY = selection.anchorY ?? selection.height / 2;
