utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const floodFill = (
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  startX: number,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  startY: number,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  fillColorHex: string,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  opacity: number = 1,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  tolerance: number = 0
utils/drawingUtils.ts:) => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const canvas = ctx.canvas;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const width = canvas.width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const height = canvas.height;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (startX < 0 || startY < 0 || startX >= width || startY >= height) return;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Get image data
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const imageData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const data = imageData.data;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const targetColor = getPixel(data, startX, startY, width);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const fillColor = hexToRgba(fillColorHex);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  fillColor.a = Math.round(opacity * 255);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // If colors are the same and opacity is 1, return
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (opacity === 1 && colorsMatch(targetColor, fillColor, tolerance)) return;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const queue: [number, number][] = [[startX, startY]];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const visited = new Uint8Array(width * height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Simple optimization to prevent infinite loops or huge memory usage in complex cases
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let iterations = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const maxIterations = width * height;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  while (queue.length > 0 && iterations < maxIterations) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    iterations++;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const [x, y] = queue.pop()!;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const idx = y * width + x;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (visited[idx]) continue;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    let currentX = x;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    let currentY = y;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Find left bound
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    while (currentX >= 0 && colorsMatch(getPixel(data, currentX, currentY, width), targetColor, tolerance)) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      currentX--;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    currentX++; // Step back to valid pixel
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    let spanAbove = false;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    let spanBelow = false;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Scan right
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    while (currentX < width && colorsMatch(getPixel(data, currentX, currentY, width), targetColor, tolerance)) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      setPixel(data, currentX, currentY, width, fillColor);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      visited[currentY * width + currentX] = 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      if (currentY > 0) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        const checkAbove = colorsMatch(getPixel(data, currentX, currentY - 1, width), targetColor, tolerance);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        if (!spanAbove && checkAbove && !visited[(currentY - 1) * width + currentX]) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          queue.push([currentX, currentY - 1]);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          spanAbove = true;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        } else if (spanAbove && !checkAbove) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          spanAbove = false;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      if (currentY < height - 1) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        const checkBelow = colorsMatch(getPixel(data, currentX, currentY + 1, width), targetColor, tolerance);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        if (!spanBelow && checkBelow && !visited[(currentY + 1) * width + currentX]) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          queue.push([currentX, currentY + 1]);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          spanBelow = true;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        } else if (spanBelow && !checkBelow) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:          spanBelow = false;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      currentX++;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.putImageData(imageData, 0, 0);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:};
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:/**
utils/drawingUtils.ts: * Optimized Magic Wand Selection tool using scanline-like approach or efficient BFS/DFS
utils/drawingUtils.ts: * with TypedArrays to avoid memory overhead and object allocation.
utils/drawingUtils.ts: */
utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const magicWandSelect = (
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  startX: number,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  startY: number
utils/drawingUtils.ts:): SelectionState | null => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const canvas = ctx.canvas;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const width = canvas.width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const height = canvas.height;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (startX < 0 || startY < 0 || startX >= width || startY >= height) return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const imageData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const data = imageData.data;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Use a 32-bit view for much faster pixel comparisons (RGBA as a single integer)
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const data32 = new Uint32Array(data.buffer);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const targetColor32 = data32[startY * width + startX];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const targetAlpha = (targetColor32 >> 24) & 0xFF;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Faster queue using TypedArray to avoid object creation
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const queue = new Int32Array(width * height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let head = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let tail = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  queue[tail++] = startY * width + startX;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const visited = new Uint8Array(width * height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  visited[startY * width + startX] = 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let minX = width, minY = height, maxX = 0, maxY = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let touchesEdge = false;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let pixelCount = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Comparison function that respects original transparency matching logic
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const isMatch = (pixel32: number) => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (targetAlpha === 0) return (pixel32 & 0xFF000000) === 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    return pixel32 === targetColor32;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Perform the selection flood fill
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  while (head < tail) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const idx = queue[head++];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const x = idx % width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const y = (idx / width) | 0; // Much faster than Math.floor for positive integers
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    pixelCount++;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (x < minX) minX = x;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (y < minY) minY = y;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (x === 0 || x === width - 1 || y === 0 || y === height - 1) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      touchesEdge = true;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Neighbors (4-connectivity)
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Up
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (y > 0) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      const upIdx = idx - width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      if (!visited[upIdx] && isMatch(data32[upIdx])) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        visited[upIdx] = 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        queue[tail++] = upIdx;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Down
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (y < height - 1) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      const downIdx = idx + width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      if (!visited[downIdx] && isMatch(data32[downIdx])) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        visited[downIdx] = 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        queue[tail++] = downIdx;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Left
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (x > 0) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      const leftIdx = idx - 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      if (!visited[leftIdx] && isMatch(data32[leftIdx])) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        visited[leftIdx] = 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        queue[tail++] = leftIdx;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Right
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    if (x < width - 1) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      const rightIdx = idx + 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      if (!visited[rightIdx] && isMatch(data32[rightIdx])) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        visited[rightIdx] = 1;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:        queue[tail++] = rightIdx;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // If selecting transparent/empty space and it touches the canvas edge, 
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // it means it's not fully enclosed by an outline.
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (targetAlpha === 0 && touchesEdge) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (pixelCount === 0) return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const selWidth = (maxX - minX + 1);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const selHeight = (maxY - minY + 1);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  tempCanvas.width = selWidth;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  tempCanvas.height = selHeight;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: true });
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (!tempCtx) return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const selImageData = tempCtx.createImageData(selWidth, selHeight);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const selData32 = new Uint32Array(selImageData.data.buffer);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const maskCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  maskCanvas.width = selWidth;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  maskCanvas.height = selHeight;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const maskCtx = maskCanvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: true });
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (!maskCtx) return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const maskImageData = maskCtx.createImageData(selWidth, selHeight);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const maskData32 = new Uint32Array(maskImageData.data.buffer);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Transfer pixels to the new canvas and clear from original in one loop
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // We use the queue as our list of selected pixel indices
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  for (let i = 0; i < tail; i++) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const idx = queue[i];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const x = idx % width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const y = (idx / width) | 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    const selIdx = (y - minY) * selWidth + (x - minX);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Copy to selection
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    selData32[selIdx] = data32[idx];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Set mask (black #000000, alpha 255)
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Little-endian RGBA is 0xFF000000 (A=255, B=0, G=0, R=0)
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskData32[selIdx] = 0xFF000000;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    // Clear from original
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data32[idx] = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Finalize canvases
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.putImageData(imageData, 0, 0);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  tempCtx.putImageData(selImageData, 0, 0);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  maskCtx.putImageData(maskImageData, 0, 0);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  return {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    x: minX,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    y: minY,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    width: selWidth,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    height: selHeight,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    dataUrl: tempCanvas.toDataURL(),
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskUrl: maskCanvas.toDataURL(),
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    rotation: 0,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    scaleX: 1,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    scaleY: 1,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    anchorX: selWidth / 2,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    anchorY: selHeight / 2,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    selectionType: 'wand'
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:};
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:export const lassoSelect = (
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  points: { x: number; y: number }[]
utils/drawingUtils.ts:): SelectionState | null => {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (points.length < 3) return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let minX = points[0].x;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let minY = points[0].y;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let maxX = points[0].x;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  let maxY = points[0].y;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  for (const p of points) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    minX = Math.min(minX, p.x);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const width = Math.ceil(maxX - minX);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const height = Math.ceil(maxY - minY);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const selectionCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  selectionCanvas.width = width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  selectionCanvas.height = height;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const sCtx = selectionCanvas.getContext('2d');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (!sCtx) return null;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  sCtx.beginPath();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  sCtx.moveTo(points[0].x - minX, points[0].y - minY);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    sCtx.lineTo(points[i].x - minX, points[i].y - minY);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  sCtx.closePath();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  sCtx.clip();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  sCtx.drawImage(ctx.canvas, minX, minY, width, height, 0, 0, width, height);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const dataUrl = selectionCanvas.toDataURL();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const maskCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  maskCanvas.width = width;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  maskCanvas.height = height;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const maskCtx = maskCanvas.getContext('2d');
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (maskCtx) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskCtx.beginPath();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskCtx.moveTo(points[0].x - minX, points[0].y - minY);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:      maskCtx.lineTo(points[i].x - minX, points[i].y - minY);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskCtx.closePath();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskCtx.fillStyle = '#000';
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskCtx.fill();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.save();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.beginPath();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.moveTo(points[0].x, points[0].y);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    ctx.lineTo(points[i].x, points[i].y);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.closePath();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.fill();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  ctx.restore();
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  return {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    x: minX,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    y: minY,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    width,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    height,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    dataUrl,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    rotation: 0,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    scaleX: 1,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    scaleY: 1,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    anchorX: width / 2,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    anchorY: height / 2,
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    selectionType: 'lasso',
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    maskUrl: maskCanvas.toDataURL(),
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:};
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:function getPixel(data: Uint8ClampedArray, x: number, y: number, width: number) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const index = (y * width + x) * 4;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  return {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    r: data[index],
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    g: data[index + 1],
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    b: data[index + 2],
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    a: data[index + 3],
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  };
utils/drawingUtils.ts:}
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:function setPixel(data: Uint8ClampedArray, x: number, y: number, width: number, color: { r: number, g: number, b: number, a: number }) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const index = (y * width + x) * 4;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (color.a === 255) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index] = color.r;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index + 1] = color.g;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index + 2] = color.b;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index + 3] = 255;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    return;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const bgR = data[index];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const bgG = data[index + 1];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const bgB = data[index + 2];
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const bgA = data[index + 3] / 255;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const alpha = color.a / 255;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const invAlpha = 1 - alpha;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const outA = alpha + bgA * invAlpha;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (outA === 0) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index] = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index + 1] = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index + 2] = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    data[index + 3] = 0;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    return;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  data[index] = (color.r * alpha + bgR * bgA * invAlpha) / outA;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  data[index + 1] = (color.g * alpha + bgG * bgA * invAlpha) / outA;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  data[index + 2] = (color.b * alpha + bgB * bgA * invAlpha) / outA;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  data[index + 3] = outA * 255;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:}
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:function colorsMatch(c1: { r: number, g: number, b: number, a: number }, c2: { r: number, g: number, b: number, a: number }, tolerance: number = 0) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (c1.a === 0 && c2.a === 0) return true;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  if (tolerance === 0) {
utils/drawingUtils.ts:    return c1.r === c2.r && c1.g === c2.g && c1.b === c2.b && c1.a === c2.a;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  }
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const rDiff = Math.abs(c1.r - c2.r);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const gDiff = Math.abs(c1.g - c2.g);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const bDiff = Math.abs(c1.b - c2.b);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const aDiff = Math.abs(c1.a - c2.a);
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  // Convert tolerance from 0-100 to 0-255
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  const t = (tolerance / 100) * 255;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  
utils/drawingUtils.ts:  return rDiff <= t && gDiff <= t && bDiff <= t && aDiff <= t;
utils/drawingUtils.ts:}
